___ का . प्रा . 2 32. भारत सरकार के संयुक्त सचिव ने जिस 
विदेशी मुद्रा संरक्षण और तस्करी निवारण अधिनियम , 
1974 ( 1374 का 52 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के 
अधीन आदेश फा . सं . 673/ 23/ 97 - सी . ए . 8 दिनांक 
11- 2 - 1997 को यह निदेश जारी किया था कि श्री बलवीर 
कुमार , पुत्र श्री मोहिन्द्र पाल , निवासी गली नं . 3 सभास 
नगर, फगवाड़ा ( पंजाब ) गांव पेंदी, पुलिस स्टेशन गोराया , 
को निरुद्ध कर लिया जाए और केन्द्रीय कारागार , कपरथल्ला 
( पंजाब ) में अभिरक्षा में रखा जाए ताकि उसे भविष्य में 
विदेशी मद्रा के संवर्धन के प्रतिकुल कार्य करने से रोका जा 
मके । 

2. केन्द्रीय सरकार के पास यह विषयाम करने का 
कारण है कि पक्ति व्यक्ति फरार हो गया है या अपने को छिपा 
रहा है जिसमें उक्त आदेश का निष्पादन नहीं हो सके । 


3. अतः अब केन्द्रीय मरकार , उक्त अधिनियम , की 
धारा 7 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खंड ( ख ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए, यह निर्देश देती है कि पूर्वोक्त 
व्यक्ति इस आदेश के शासकीय राजपत्र में प्रकाशन के 7 
दिन के भीतर कमिशनर ग्राफ पग्लिस , जालघर के समक्ष 
हाजिर हों ।

अवधि के दौरान धारा 11 की उपधारा ( 5 ) 
में विनिर्दिष्ट किसी एक अथवा एक से अधिक ढंग

( जेवर -जवाहिरात , फर्मीपर आदि के रूप में प्राप्त

सकेगा ;

आभलाभ के रूप में हो जब तक कि ऐसा कारो

(ii ) कर-निर्धारिती उपर उल्लिखित कर-मिर्धारण वर्षों 

से संगत पूर्ववर्ती वर्षों की किसी भी अवधि के दौरान

में स्वैच्छिक अंशदान से भिम्न ) का निवेश नहीं 

करेगा अथवा उसे जमा नहीं करवा सकेगा ;

1998- 99 तक के लिए निम्नलिखित शतों के अधीन रहते

से संगत पूर्ववर्ती वर्षों की किसी भी अवधि के दौरान 
धारा 11 की उप - धारा ( 6 ) में विनिविष्ट रिसी 
एक प्रश्रवा एक से अधिक लंग अथवा तरीकों से भिन्न

एतदद्वारा " दी देहली पारसी अंजमन नई, दिल्ली " को कर 
निर्धारण वर्ष 1991-92 से 1983- 84 तक के लिए निम्न 
लिखित प्रतों के अधीन रहते हुए उषत उपखंड के प्रयो 
जनार्थ अधिसूचित करती है, प्रत्: - - 
(i ) करनिर्धारिती इसकी प्राय का इस्तेमाल अथवा 

इसकी प्राय का इस्तेमाल करने के लिए इसका 
संचयन पूर्णतया तथा अनन्यतया उन उपयों के लिए 

करेगा, जिनके लिए इसकी स्थापना की गई है । 
( ii ) कर-निर्धारिती उ. पर - उल्लिखित कर-निर्धारण वर्षों 

से संगत पूर्ववर्ती वर्षों की किसी भी अवधि के 
वौरान धारा 11 की उपधारा ( 5 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट विसी

( iii ) यह अधिसूचना किसी ऐसी आय के संबंध में 

लाग नहीं होगी जी कि कारोबार से प्राप्त लाभ 
तथा अभिलाभ के रूप में हो जब तक ऐसा 
कारोबार उक्त कर-निर्धारिती के उद्देश्यों की 
प्राप्ति के लिए प्रासंगिक नहीं हो तथा ऐसे 
कारोबार के संबंध में अलग से लेखा- पुस्तिकाएं 
नहीं रखी जाती हों । 


तक के लिए निम्नलिखित शर्तों के अधीम रहते हुए जक्त 
उपखंड के प्रयोजनार्थ अधिसूचित करती है , अर्थात:- - 
( i ) कर -निर्धारिती हमकी प्राय का इस्तेमाल प्रथया 

इसकी आय का इस्तेमाल करने के लिए इसका 
संचयन पूर्णतया तथा अनन्यतया उन उद्देश्यों के 
लिए करेगा , जिनके लिए इसकी स्थापना की 

गई है । 
ii ) कर-निर्धारिती ऊपर उल्लिखित कर-निर्धारण 

वर्षों से संगत पूर्ववर्ती वर्षों की किसी भी अवधि 
के दौरान धारा 11 की उपधारा ( 5 ) में 
विनिर्दिष्ट किसी एक अथवा एक से अधिक 
हंग अथवा तरीकों से भिन्न तरीकों से इसकी 
निधि ( मेवर - जवाहिरात , फर्नीचर आदि के रूप

has got right to refuse it . Further as the concerned 
Warkmen had absented without grant of casual leave 
the management has got every right to deduct one 
day Salary on the basis of principle of no work no 
pay. There was no need to hold enquiry . From the 
own showing of the bank it become clear that leave 
was not refused on 20 - 2 - 93 . Instead it was refused 
On 22 -2 -93 by pasting notice on the notice board . If 
the bank had taken decision on 20 - 2 -93 itself, the 
consequences would have different. Further under 
para 19 . 7 ( a ) of Bipartite Settlement absent without 
leave amount is minor misconduct and deduction of 
one day pay is the punishment. As such without 
holding any enquiry under para 19 . 12 B . P .S . such 
punishment could not have been awarded . This 
amount to breach of this provision of settlement as 
well as violation of principle of natural justice . 

7 . Hence my award is that deduction of one day 
salary of the concerned workmen is bad in law , Con 
sequently they will be entitled for salary for one day , 
My further award is that the action of the concerned 
workmen in taking casual leave after joining the duties 
amount to mass casual leave .

2 . Although this case was reserved for giving 
finding on preliminary issuc , final award is being given 
as the punishment awarded to the concerned workman 
is less than dismissal, removal or discharge from 
service and further that this tribunal cannot examine 
the proportionality of the punishment in exercise of 
its powers under sec. 11 - A of 1. D . Act, and further 
that the domestic enquiry is ultimately going to be 
held as fair and proper.

nc suhmitted his report dated 30 -10 - 90 . Agreeing with

page of two increments with cumulative effect. Feeling 
agerieved the concerned workman has raised the instant 
industrial dispute .

4 . The opposite party has filed reply in which it is allceed 
that actually 14 -8 - 95 was fixed for interview , docording to 
his own showing the concerned workman had rocovered from 
his illness from 13 -8 - 95 oven then if he did not appear in 
intervicw on 14 - 8 - 95 , the management bank was perfectly 
within its right to debar the applicant from benefit of çir . 
cular for a period of three years. 


5. In the rejoinder nothing new has been proved . 

6 . Thus the only point which is crucial for determination 
in the case is , as to whether the date of interyiew was 
11 - 8 -95 of 14 -8 -95 as alleged by the management. Thero 
is evidence of G . K . Mishra M . W . I that dato of interview 


4308 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : NOVEMBER 14 , 1998 +KARTIKA 23 , 1920 


[PART II- $ 6c . ( 11 ) ] 


wag 14 . 8 - 95 . No doubt date was fixed for 11 - 8 - 95 . On 
the other hand the concerned workman has stated that 
11 - 8 -95 was fixed for interview and on this pretext he had 
taken advance as well. In this case in my opinion oral 
evidence is of no consequence . The matcnal docunent will 
be the lotter of interview which was issued by the manage 
ment which is in his possession . In such case manupula 
tion of date of interview would not arise . The conccined 
workman has filed interview letter . It shows that originally 
11- 8 - 95 was cyclostyled . Lateron it has been altered as 
14 - 8 - 95 . It appears that the concerned workman is trying 
to take benefit of alteration of 11th by 14th . As sald 
earlier this letter was in possession of the concerned work 
man . It is not the case of the workman that subsequently 
the figure 11 has been altcted into 14 . It will be deemed 
that this letter was delivered to the concomed workman in 
altered way and on the basis of this letter my finding is that 
actually 14 - 8 - 99 was fixed for interview as the workman 
himself had not appeared after recovery from the alleged 
illnesy on 13- 8 - 95 , hence tho opposito party was perfectly 
justified in not considering his claim and further debarring 
him by passing the inpugned order . Consequently my award 
is that impugncd order passed by the bank was justified and 
consequently the workman is not entitled for any relief.

3. The opposite party bank has filed reply in which it has 
becn alleged that the concerned workman was not engaged On 
Any permanent post, Instead he was engaged on daily rated 
worker to do the work in the temporary absence of permanent 
employee. Ho is not entitled for regularisation as he was 
not engaged in any capacity . Further he is not entitled for 
Rs. 400 as salary 

4 . th the rejoinder nothing new has been leged . 

$. There is inherrent conflict between reference and plead 
ing regarding date of termination . According to reference 
he was removed from gorvice on 12 -8 - 95 whereas according 
to claim statoment he was removed from sorvice in March 
1986 . In view of this discripancy the date of termination as 
given in reference is bad as according to own version the 
concerned workman was service on 12 - 8 - 1995 , henge quer 
tion of termipation being bad does not asise . 


6 . As the concerned workman is not in service , question of 
his regularisation cannot be considered . 

7 . As regards the claim for Rs. 400 per month jastead , of 
Rs. 5 per day there is evidence of Jaipal Reddi M . W . I that 
the concerned workman was used to be engaged for 15 to 20 
minutos as and when necessity arose . This fact has not been 
denied by the concerned workman . W . W . 1 In my cpinion 
when a part time mweeper works for 15 to 20 minutes he is 
not cutice for any regular pay. Hence according to the 
provision of Bipartite Settlement he is not entitled for Rs. 400 
per month 
8 . In view of bbove my award is that the termination of 
the workman is not bad in 1:1w and he is not entitled for 
any relief.

9 . From the facts admitted by the partios in their written

as Category E Head Cashior was not justified .

11 . In the aforesaid circumstances the present dispute 
being restricted to the refusal of the management to 
consider the case of Shri Dave before appointment of Shri 
Sheth to the post of Category E Head Cashier in the 
Braboume Road Branch on his transfer , it is necessary 
to cusider the connected ygrecident /settlement in respect 
ot his meiter. I have already stated that both the rarties 
have produced bipartite settlement dated 4 -6 . 1988 and there 
is no disptue botwen the parties that they will be governed 
by the terms of the settlopent in sofar as it lays down 
the procedure for appointment to the post of Category E 
Cashier . On an analysis of the provisions laid down therein 
it will appear that the Regional Manager shall have to 
call for applications from the Head Cashier /Paving Cashier 
Category C from the cotre or from tho Category A 
if the Category C Cashier is not available and the senior 
most of these anplications shall be considered for this 
allowance . The otin. requisites for such appointment 
is that the applicant s rot to go out of the Cash Depart 
ment voluntarily and that he is not to relinquish cash 
allowance in any category voluntarily . A clarification WAS 
made by the Bank in circular dated 21 - 11 - 1988 wherein 
it was stated that Category C and Category E allowance 
shall be admissible to those branches which are large and 
very largo respectively . It was further provided that ampest

12 . It is accordingly necessary to consider whether the 
Bank has complied with the terms of the aforesaid settle 
ment. The management has taken the plea that Shri Sheth 
being the senis ,most imongst the three that the manage 
moet directly posted him as Category E Cashier in the 
Brabuline koad B1 .rii aster transferring his servicc front 
Park Circus Branch as Category A Cashier. Seniority of 
service of Shri Sheth is not disputed in so far as it relates 
to the respective dates of appointment of each one of them . 
It was however alleged thut such seniority for appointment 
in Category Head Cashier is lost due to the voluntary 
relinguistument of spocial allowance on his transfer to 
Cuchilla . The mina penent bas vehimently denied that it 
was it use of voluntary relinguishment, but the Bank in 
it wishdom declined to grant him any special allowance 
as Chi - gory A Cashier because of his prayer for transfer 
to Calcutta and that on his appointment as Category E 
Cashier his special allowance to the said post is being paid . 


13 . As stated above , the point for consideration being 
whether due compliance was made to the provisions of 
the settlement for appointment of Category E Cashier , 
I find nv compliance was made in respect of the same. As 
stated earlier, lur jiliing any vacancy of Head Cashier 
Category E it was necessary for the Regional Manager to 
call for the applications from the centre or from Category 
A Cashiers , if Category C Head Cashier is not avajlable . 
It was therefore the duty of the Regional Manager to call 
for the applications from Category C Head Cashiers ini 
tially . There is nothing on record to show that Category 
C Herd Cashier was not available for appointment . Appli 
titili 10m Casner Cate2005 A con be called for only 
if Category C Head Cashier is not available. Appoint 
ment to Head Cashier Category E in this case has admitted !y 
been made on the basis of seniority at Service of Shri Sheth , 
Hc was admittedly a Category A Cashier like Shri Dave 
and Shri Mandal at the time of his appointment. In the 
instant casc , it is admitted that ng application was called 
for riilk from Category Chicad Cashier or from Category 
A Cashiers for filling up the vacant post of Category " E 
Head Cashier. The 21 pointinent of Shri Sheth accordingly 
Was illegal and void since he was not appointed upon 
compliance of the basic provisions for such appointment, 
Under Section 18 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 the 
parties are bound to enly the terms of the settlement. It 
in an established frinciple of law that if any parti 
cular mode is prescribed for appointment to a particular 
post in a particular manner as prescribed under law , the 
act must be performed in the said manner or not at all, 
" The management therefore cannot he allowed to justify 
such appointment of Shri Sheth on the basis of his senjority . 
There are also other things for corsideration as stated earlier 
by me, before appointment to the said post. Admittedly . 
no such consideration is made by the management as it 
will spoor from the cvidence of MW - 1 in the matter . 
Appointment of Shri S . ( ). Sheth to the said post must 
accordingly be held to be illegal Since arpointment of 
Shri Sheth is found to be illegal and void , there is no 
score for finding wheths by making such appointment 
the management ignore the claims of Shri Dave and 
Shri Mandal

and station leuve cannot be granted and her absence from 
duties will be treated as unauthorised , and bank will take 
appropriate disciplinary action against her violating leave 


10 . In the result, in view of my discussions and finding 
above as a whole , I find and hold that the workman , 
Mrs. Meena Chakravarty is cntitled to relicf of joining in her 
service only without any benefit i.e. back wages, seniority , 
othor amonities ctc . The management is directed to allow 
the worknun , Mrs. Meena Chakravarty, to join in her service 
at Tarapur Branch of UBT, Silchar . But she cannot claim 
any benefit other amenity whatsoevei from the nuagement 
as mentioned above . Her benefit of service wages etc . will be 
counted with effect from the date of her joining only at 
Tarapus Branch of UBI, Silchar . 


11 , I, therefore , give my award on this the 25th day of 
September, 1998 in above terms, and the reference is disposed 
of without cost. 

D . HAZARIKA, Presiding Officer
